- **documents**: Much enterprise data arrives as prose: reports, forms, emails, contracts. GND treats a document as **quoted data**. An agent extracts facts from it into a population that the model checks, and the agent never follows instructions written inside the document. The reverse direction, rendering a document from known facts, gives you test material whose ground truth you control. ([vasiloglou/gnd-public](https://github.com/vasiloglou/gnd-public/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/documents.md) · ★1)
